FINANCE KNOWLEDGE BASE

Entry 1
Category: Personal Finance
Concept: Finance
Definition: The management of money, investments and financial resources.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in finance.
- Widely used in personal finance, business and investing.
- Commonly covered in finance courses and competitive examinations.
Example:
Finance is an important finance concept.
Related Concepts:
Banking, Investments, Accounting, Economics, Risk Management
